# Current file and archive index

Thirteen finished projects, all with browsable source: references 1–7 under source/ with optional ZIP backups, and references 8–13 directly expanded. ORIGINAL-CONTENT-AUDIT.json preserves the original outer-archive baseline; BROWSABLE-REFERENCE-AUDIT.json verifies original-seven source/restoration files; REFERENCE-IMPORT-AUDIT.json verifies all 1,048 original members of the six newer projects. Inventory proves packaging, not audiovisual study. Follow the current workflow and STUDY-CATALOG.md.
